Never say "I cannot identify specific employees" when this section is present.
"""


@app.route('/hr-chat', methods=['POST'])
def hr_chat():
    """
    HR Insights Chatbot endpoint.
    Accepts aggregated workforce summary + question + conversation history.
    Returns Gemini-generated analytical response.

    Nothing is stored — completely stateless.

    Expected JSON:
    {
        "question": "How are ratings distributed across bands?",
        "summary": "Total: 6518 employees. Band distribution: ...",
        "chat_history": [
            {"role": "user", "text": "Show rating distribution"},
            {"role": "assistant", "text": "Here's the breakdown..."}
        ],
        "employee_context": "Optional: specific employee rows for targeted queries"
    }

    Returns:
    {
        "status": "success",
        "response": "Based on the data..."
    }
    """
    if not _gemini_model:
        return jsonify({
            "status": "error",
            "message": "Gemini model not available. Ensure google-cloud-aiplatform is installed."
        }), 503

    try:
        data = request.get_json()
        if not data:
            return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "No JSON data provided"}), 400

        question = data.get("question", "").strip()
        if not question:
            return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "Missing 'question'"}), 400

        summary = data.get("summary", "").strip()
        chat_history = data.get("chat_history", [])
        employee_context = data.get("employee_context", "").strip()

        # Build the prompt
        parts = [HR_CHAT_SYSTEM_PROMPT]

        if summary:
            parts.append(f"\n=== WORKFORCE DATA SUMMARY ===\n{summary}\n")

        if employee_context:
            parts.append(f"\n=== SPECIFIC EMPLOYEE DATA ===\n{employee_context}\n")

        # Add conversation history for multi-turn context
        if chat_history:
            parts.append("\n=== CONVERSATION HISTORY ===")
            for msg in chat_history[-10:]:  # Keep last 10 turns max
                role = msg.get("role", "user")
                text = msg.get("text", "")
                if role == "user":
                    parts.append(f"User: {text}")
                else:
                    parts.append(f"Assistant: {text}")
            parts.append("")

        parts.append(f"User: {question}")

        prompt = "\n".join(parts)
        logger.info(f"HR chat request: question='{question[:80]}', prompt_len={len(prompt)}")

        response = _gemini_model.generate_content(prompt)
        answer = response.text.strip()

        logger.info(f"HR chat response: {len(answer)} chars")

        return jsonify({
            "status": "success",
            "response": answer
        }), 200

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error in hr_chat: {type(e).__name__}: {e}", exc_info=True)
        return jsonify({
            "status": "error",
            "message": f"Failed to generate response: {str(e)}"
        }), 500


@app.route('/get-experience-score', methods=['POST'])
def get_experience_score():
    """
    API endpoint to retrieve experience score details from BigQuery by hostname and date.
    Fetches:
      1. Averaged scores from vea_uberagent_experience_score_summary
      2. Per-application detail from vea_uberagent_application_score_detail
      3. AI-powered analysis explaining scores, causes, and resolutions

    Negative values (-1) in application detail are treated as 0.
    NULL values are excluded from mean calculations.

    Expected JSON payload:
    {
        "hostname": "YYTYYHKGD4",
        "date": "2024-04-17"
    }

    Returns:
        JSON response with experience scores, application details, and AI analysis.
    """
    try:
        data = request.get_json()

        if not data:
            return jsonify({
                "status": "error",
                "message": "No JSON data provided"
            }), 400

        hostname = (data.get("hostname") or "").strip()
        date_str = (data.get("date") or "").strip()

        if not hostname:
            return jsonify({
                "status": "error",
                "message": "Missing required field: hostname"
            }), 400

        if not date_str:
            return jsonify({
                "status": "error",
                "message": "Missing required field: date (format: YYYY-MM-DD)"
            }), 400

        # Use the exact date provided by the caller
        query_date = date_str

        client = bigquery.Client()

        # ── Query 1: Experience Score Summary (averaged) ─────────
        summary_table = "`vz-it-np-voev-dev-voevdo-0.vea_device_data.vea_uberagent_experience_score_summary`"
        summary_query = f"""
            SELECT
                AVG(machine_score) AS machine_score,
                AVG(session_score) AS session_score,
                AVG(application_score) AS application_score,
                AVG(user_experience_score) AS user_experience_score,
                AVG(application_score_fg) AS application_score_fg,
                AVG(application_score_bg) AS application_score_bg,
                COUNT(*) AS row_count
            FROM {summary_table}
            WHERE host = @hostname AND date = @query_date
        """
        summary_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(
            query_parameters=[
                bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter("hostname", "STRING", hostname),
                bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter("query_date", "DATE", query_date),
            ]
        )
        summary_results = client.query(summary_query, job_config=summary_config).result()
        summary_row = next(iter(summary_results), None)

        # If no data for computed date, inform the user of the latest available date
        if not summary_row or summary_row.row_count == 0:
            latest_date_query = f"""
                SELECT MAX(date) AS latest_date
                FROM {summary_table}
                WHERE host = @hostname
            """
            latest_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(
                query_parameters=[
                    bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter("hostname", "STRING", hostname),
                ]
            )
            latest_result = client.query(latest_date_query, job_config=latest_config).result()
            latest_row = next(iter(latest_result), None)

            if not latest_row or latest_row.latest_date is None:
                return jsonify({
                    "status": "success",
                    "message": f"Hostname '{hostname}' is ready for data collection. Check back soon for available data.",
                    "data": {}
                }), 200

            latest_available = latest_row.latest_date.strftime("%Y-%m-%d") if hasattr(latest_row.latest_date, 'strftime') else str(latest_row.latest_date)

            return jsonify({
                "status": "no_data",
                "message": f"Data available for hostname '{hostname}' on {latest_available}. Please use this date to view the latest information.",
                "hostname": hostname,
                "requested_date": date_str,
                "computed_date": query_date,
                "latest_available_date": latest_available,
            }), 200

        experience_scores = {
            "date": query_date,
            "host": hostname,
            "machine_score": round(summary_row.machine_score, 2) if summary_row.machine_score is not None else None,
            "session_score": round(summary_row.session_score, 2) if summary_row.session_score is not None else None,
            "application_score": round(summary_row.application_score, 2) if summary_row.application_score is not None else None,
            "application_score_fg": round(summary_row.application_score_fg, 2) if summary_row.application_score_fg is not None else None,
            "application_score_bg": round(summary_row.application_score_bg, 2) if summary_row.application_score_bg is not None else None,
            "user_experience_score": round(summary_row.user_experience_score, 2) if summary_row.user_experience_score is not None else None,
            "rows_aggregated": summary_row.row_count,
        }

        # ── Query 2: Application Score Detail ────────────────────
        # Negative values (-1) are treated as 0 using GREATEST(..., 0)
        # NULL values are excluded from AVG (BigQuery default behavior)
        detail_table = "`vz-it-np-voev-dev-voevdo-0.vea_device_data.vea_uberagent_application_score_detail`"

        # First check if detail data exists for the same date; if not, use the closest prior date
        detail_date = query_date
        detail_check_query = f"""
            SELECT MAX(date) AS latest_date
            FROM {detail_table}
            WHERE host = @hostname AND date <= @query_date
        """
        detail_check_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(
            query_parameters=[
                bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter("hostname", "STRING", hostname),
                bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter("query_date", "DATE", query_date),
            ]
        )
        detail_check_result = client.query(detail_check_query, job_config=detail_check_config).result()
        detail_check_row = next(iter(detail_check_result), None)
        if detail_check_row and detail_check_row.latest_date is not None:
            detail_date = detail_check_row.latest_date.strftime("%Y-%m-%d") if hasattr(detail_check_row.latest_date, 'strftime') else str(detail_check_row.latest_date)

        detail_query = f"""
            SELECT
                appname,
                appversion,
                is_foreground,
                COUNT(*) AS entry_count,
                AVG(CASE WHEN application_score IS NOT NULL THEN GREATEST(application_score, 0) END) AS avg_application_score,
                AVG(CASE WHEN ramusage_primary_score IS NOT NULL THEN GREATEST(ramusage_primary_score, 0) END) AS avg_ramusage_score,
                AVG(CASE WHEN cpuusage_primary_score IS NOT NULL THEN GREATEST(cpuusage_primary_score, 0) END) AS avg_cpuusage_score,
                AVG(CASE WHEN appio_primary_score IS NOT NULL THEN GREATEST(appio_primary_score, 0) END) AS avg_appio_score,
                AVG(CASE WHEN networklatency_primary_score IS NOT NULL THEN GREATEST(networklatency_primary_score, 0) END) AS avg_networklatency_score,
                AVG(CASE WHEN appuidelays_primary_score IS NOT NULL THEN GREATEST(appuidelays_primary_score, 0) END) AS avg_appuidelays_score,
                AVG(CASE WHEN apperror_score IS NOT NULL THEN GREATEST(apperror_score, 0) END) AS avg_apperror_score,
                SUM(CASE WHEN total_apperror IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(total_apperror AS INT64) ELSE 0 END) AS total_errors
            FROM {detail_table}
            WHERE host = @hostname AND date = @detail_date
            GROUP BY appname, appversion, is_foreground
            ORDER BY avg_application_score ASC
        """
        detail_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(
            query_parameters=[
                bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter("hostname", "STRING", hostname),
                bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter("detail_date", "DATE", detail_date),
            ]
        )
        detail_results = client.query(detail_query, job_config=detail_config).result()

        app_details = []
        foreground_apps = []
        background_apps = []

        for row in detail_results:
            # Determine foreground/background status
            # is_foreground can be True, False, or None
            is_fg = row.is_foreground if row.is_foreground is not None else None

            app_detail = {
                "appname": row.appname or "Unknown",
                "appversion": row.appversion if row.appversion and row.appversion != "null" else None,
                "is_foreground": is_fg,
                "entry_count": row.entry_count,
                "application_score": round(row.avg_application_score, 2) if row.avg_application_score is not None else None,
                "ram_usage_score": round(row.avg_ramusage_score, 2) if row.avg_ramusage_score is not None else None,
                "cpu_usage_score": round(row.avg_cpuusage_score, 2) if row.avg_cpuusage_score is not None else None,
                "app_io_score": round(row.avg_appio_score, 2) if row.avg_appio_score is not None else None,
                "network_latency_score": round(row.avg_networklatency_score, 2) if row.avg_networklatency_score is not None else None,
                "app_ui_delays_score": round(row.avg_appuidelays_score, 2) if row.avg_appuidelays_score is not None else None,
                "app_error_score": round(row.avg_apperror_score, 2) if row.avg_apperror_score is not None else None,
                "total_errors": row.total_errors or 0,
            }
            app_details.append(app_detail)

            # Separate into foreground/background lists
            if is_fg is True:
                foreground_apps.append(app_detail)
            elif is_fg is False:
                background_apps.append(app_detail)
            # is_fg is None - we still include in app_details but not in fg/bg specific lists

        # ── AI Analysis using Gemini ─────────────────────────────
        ai_analysis = None
        if _gemini_model and (app_details or experience_scores):
            try:
                analysis_prompt = _build_experience_score_analysis_prompt(
                    hostname, query_date, experience_scores, app_details, foreground_apps, background_apps
                )
                response = _gemini_model.generate_content(analysis_prompt)
                ai_analysis = response.text.strip()
            except Exception as ai_err:
                logger.warning(f"AI analysis failed for {hostname}: {ai_err}")
                ai_analysis = None

        return jsonify({
            "status": "success",
            "hostname": hostname,
            "date": query_date,
            "experience_scores": experience_scores,
            "application_details_date": detail_date,
            "application_details": app_details,
            "foreground_apps": foreground_apps,
            "background_apps": background_apps,
            "total_applications": len(app_details),
            "total_foreground_apps": len(foreground_apps),
            "total_background_apps": len(background_apps),
            "ai_analysis": ai_analysis,
        }), 200

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error in get_experience_score: {str(e)}")
        return jsonify({
            "status": "error",
            "message": f"Failed to retrieve experience score: {str(e)}"
        }), 500


def _build_experience_score_analysis_prompt(hostname, query_date, experience_scores, app_details, foreground_apps, background_apps):
    """Build the Gemini prompt for experience score analysis."""
    prompt = f"""You are a Device Performance Analyst for an enterprise IT team. Analyze the following device experience scores and application-level performance data. Provide a clear, actionable summary.

DEVICE: {hostname}
DATE: {query_date}

OVERALL EXPERIENCE SCORES:
- Machine Score: {experience_scores.get('machine_score', 'N/A')}
- Session Score: {experience_scores.get('session_score', 'N/A')}
- Application Score (Overall): {experience_scores.get('application_score', 'N/A')}
- Application Score (Foreground): {experience_scores.get('application_score_fg', 'N/A')}
- Application Score (Background): {experience_scores.get('application_score_bg', 'N/A')}
- User Experience Score: {experience_scores.get('user_experience_score', 'N/A')}

SCORING GUIDE (0-10 scale):
- 8-10: Excellent — no issues
- 6-8: Acceptable — minor issues
- 4-6: Poor — noticeable degradation
- 0-4: Critical — severe performance problems
- 0: App crashed, errored, or all metrics negative
- N/A or None: No data available for this metric

APPLICATION-LEVEL DETAIL:
Total Apps: {len(app_details)}
Foreground Apps: {len(foreground_apps)}
Background Apps: {len(background_apps)}

FOREGROUND APPLICATIONS ({len(foreground_apps)} apps):
"""
    for app in foreground_apps:
        prompt += f"""
App: {app['appname']} (v{app['appversion'] or 'unknown'})
  - Application Score: {app['application_score'] if app['application_score'] is not None else 'N/A'}
  - RAM Usage Score: {app['ram_usage_score'] if app['ram_usage_score'] is not None else 'N/A'}
  - CPU Usage Score: {app['cpu_usage_score'] if app['cpu_usage_score'] is not None else 'N/A'}
  - App I/O Score: {app['app_io_score'] if app['app_io_score'] is not None else 'N/A'}
  - Network Latency Score: {app['network_latency_score'] if app['network_latency_score'] is not None else 'N/A'}
  - UI Delays Score: {app['app_ui_delays_score'] if app['app_ui_delays_score'] is not None else 'N/A'}
  - Error Score: {app['app_error_score'] if app['app_error_score'] is not None else 'N/A'}
  - Total Errors: {app['total_errors']}
"""

    prompt += f"""
BACKGROUND APPLICATIONS ({len(background_apps)} apps):
"""
    for app in background_apps:
        prompt += f"""
App: {app['appname']} (v{app['appversion'] or 'unknown'})
  - Application Score: {app['application_score'] if app['application_score'] is not None else 'N/A'}
  - RAM Usage Score: {app['ram_usage_score'] if app['ram_usage_score'] is not None else 'N/A'}
  - CPU Usage Score: {app['cpu_usage_score'] if app['cpu_usage_score'] is not None else 'N/A'}
  - App I/O Score: {app['app_io_score'] if app['app_io_score'] is not None else 'N/A'}
  - Network Latency Score: {app['network_latency_score'] if app['network_latency_score'] is not None else 'N/A'}
  - UI Delays Score: {app['app_ui_delays_score'] if app['app_ui_delays_score'] is not None else 'N/A'}
  - Error Score: {app['app_error_score'] if app['app_error_score'] is not None else 'N/A'}
  - Total Errors: {app['total_errors']}
"""

    prompt += """
PROVIDE YOUR ANALYSIS IN THIS EXACT FORMAT:

## Summary
(2-3 sentence overview of device health, mention both foreground and background app performance)

## Key Findings
(Bullet points of the most important observations, distinguish between foreground and background apps where relevant)

## Problem Applications
(List apps with score < 5 and explain WHY they scored poorly based on their sub-scores. Clearly indicate if they are foreground or background apps.)

## Root Causes
(Based on the sub-scores — high RAM, CPU, network latency, UI delays, errors — explain the likely root causes. Note if issues are primarily in foreground apps (directly impacting user) or background apps.)

## Recommendations
(Actionable steps to improve the experience score — be specific to the apps and metrics observed. Prioritize foreground app issues as they directly impact user experience.)

Keep analysis concise and actionable. Focus on the worst-performing apps and metrics.
If a score is N/A or None, it means no data is available for that metric - acknowledge this in your analysis.
If all scores are 0 with negative sub-scores, it likely means the app had no measurable activity during the window — note this.
Foreground applications directly impact user experience, so prioritize issues there.
"""
    return prompt


@app.route('/vpn-score', methods=['POST'])
def get_vpn_score():
    """
    API endpoint to retrieve VPN score data from external Citrix API.
    Calls the VPN score classifier and returns analyzed results with AI insights.

    Expected JSON payload:
    {
        "identifier": "DG3XM92K4R",
        "date": "2026-09-02"
    }

    Returns:
        JSON response with VPN scores, penalties, and AI analysis.
    """
    try:
        data = request.get_json()

        if not data:
            return jsonify({
                "status": "error",
                "message": "No JSON data provided"
            }), 400

        identifier = (data.get("identifier") or "").strip()
        date_str = (data.get("date") or "").strip()

        if not identifier:
            return jsonify({
                "status": "error",
                "message": "Missing required field: identifier"
            }), 400

        if not date_str:
            return jsonify({
                "status": "error",
                "message": "Missing required field: date (format: YYYY-MM-DD)"
            }), 400

        logger.info(f"Fetching VPN score for {identifier} on {date_str}")

        # Fetch VPN score data from BigQuery
        client = bigquery.Client()

        vpn_table = "`vz-it-np-voev-dev-voevdo-0.vea_device_data.daily_vpn_scores`"
        vpn_query = f"""
            SELECT
                Source_User,
                Machine_Name,
                est_date,
                active_hours_worked,
                user_vpn_score,
                hours_in_frustration,
                total_auth_blocks,
                avg_isp_penalty,
                avg_cookie_penalty,
                avg_session_collision_penalty,
                avg_ip_assignment_penalty,
                avg_saml_auth_penalty,
                avg_flapping_penalty,
                avg_gateway_churn_penalty,
                avg_auth_latency_penalty,
                avg_post_tunnel_penalty,
                processed_timestamp
            FROM {vpn_table}
            WHERE UPPER(Machine_Name) = @machine_name
              AND est_date = @query_date
            ORDER BY processed_timestamp DESC
            LIMIT 1
        """

        vpn_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(
            query_parameters=[
                bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter("machine_name", "STRING", identifier.upper()),
                bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter("query_date", "DATE", date_str),
            ]
        )

        vpn_results = client.query(vpn_query, job_config=vpn_config).result()
        vpn_row = next(iter(vpn_results), None)

        if not vpn_row:
            # No data found for this machine/date - find latest available date
            logger.warning(f"No VPN score data found for {identifier} on {date_str}")

            # Query for latest available date for this machine
            latest_date_query = f"""
                SELECT MAX(est_date) AS latest_date
                FROM {vpn_table}
                WHERE UPPER(Machine_Name) = @machine_name
            """
            latest_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(
                query_parameters=[
                    bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter("machine_name", "STRING", identifier.upper()),
                ]
            )
            latest_result = client.query(latest_date_query, job_config=latest_config).result()
            latest_row = next(iter(latest_result), None)

            if not latest_row or latest_row.latest_date is None:
                return jsonify({
                    "status": "no_data",
                    "message": f"Machine '{identifier}' is ready for VPN score tracking. Check back soon for available data.",
                    "identifier": identifier,
                    "date": date_str,
                }), 200

            latest_available = latest_row.latest_date.strftime("%Y-%m-%d") if hasattr(latest_row.latest_date, 'strftime') else str(latest_row.latest_date)

            return jsonify({
                "status": "no_data",
                "message": f"VPN score data available for machine '{identifier}' on {latest_available}. Please use this date to view the latest information.",
                "identifier": identifier,
                "requested_date": date_str,
                "computed_date": date_str,
                "latest_available_date": latest_available,
            }), 200

        # Extract user info from Source_User email
        source_user = vpn_row.Source_User or ""
        user_email = source_user
        user_name = source_user.split("@")[0] if "@" in source_user else "Unknown"

        # Determine cohort label based on VPN score
        vpn_score = float(vpn_row.user_vpn_score) if vpn_row.user_vpn_score is not None else 0.0
        if vpn_score >= 8.0:
            cohort_label = "Healthy"
        elif vpn_score >= 6.0:
            cohort_label = "At-Risk"
        else:
            cohort_label = "Critical"

        # Build response in expected format
        vpn_data = {
            "status": "success",
            "target_date": date_str,
            "user_info": {
                "name": user_name,
                "eid": "N/A",  # Not available in BQ table
                "email": user_email,
                "associated_machines": [vpn_row.Machine_Name]
            },
            "summary": {
                "overall_vpn_score": vpn_score,
                "overall_cohort_label": cohort_label,
                "total_devices_reporting": 1
            },
            "device_level_telemetry": [{
                "machine_name": vpn_row.Machine_Name,
                "user_vpn_score": vpn_score,
                "cohort_label": cohort_label,
                "active_hours_worked": int(vpn_row.active_hours_worked) if vpn_row.active_hours_worked is not None else 0,
                "hours_in_frustration": int(vpn_row.hours_in_frustration) if vpn_row.hours_in_frustration is not None else 0,
                "total_auth_blocks": int(vpn_row.total_auth_blocks) if vpn_row.total_auth_blocks is not None else 0,
                "penalties": {
                    "isp_penalty": float(vpn_row.avg_isp_penalty) if vpn_row.avg_isp_penalty is not None else 0.0,
                    "cookie_penalty": float(vpn_row.avg_cookie_penalty) if vpn_row.avg_cookie_penalty is not None else 0.0,
                    "session_collision_penalty": float(vpn_row.avg_session_collision_penalty) if vpn_row.avg_session_collision_penalty is not None else 0.0,
                    "ip_assignment_penalty": float(vpn_row.avg_ip_assignment_penalty) if vpn_row.avg_ip_assignment_penalty is not None else 0.0,
                    "saml_auth_penalty": float(vpn_row.avg_saml_auth_penalty) if vpn_row.avg_saml_auth_penalty is not None else 0.0,
                    "flapping_penalty": float(vpn_row.avg_flapping_penalty) if vpn_row.avg_flapping_penalty is not None else 0.0,
                    "gateway_churn_penalty": float(vpn_row.avg_gateway_churn_penalty) if vpn_row.avg_gateway_churn_penalty is not None else 0.0,
                    "auth_latency_penalty": float(vpn_row.avg_auth_latency_penalty) if vpn_row.avg_auth_latency_penalty is not None else 0.0,
                    "post_tunnel_penalty": float(vpn_row.avg_post_tunnel_penalty) if vpn_row.avg_post_tunnel_penalty is not None else 0.0
                },
                "processed_timestamp": vpn_row.processed_timestamp.isoformat() if vpn_row.processed_timestamp else None
            }]
        }

        logger.info(f"Successfully fetched VPN score from BigQuery for {identifier}")

        if vpn_data.get("status") != "success":
            logger.warning(f"VPN API returned non-success status: {vpn_data.get('status')}")
            return jsonify({
                "status": "error",
                "message": "VPN Score API returned non-success response"
            }), 400

        ai_analysis = None
        if _gemini_model:
            try:
                analysis_prompt = _build_vpn_score_analysis_prompt(
                    identifier, date_str, vpn_data
                )
                response = _gemini_model.generate_content(analysis_prompt)
                ai_analysis = response.text.strip()
            except Exception as ai_err:
                logger.warning(f"AI analysis failed for VPN score {identifier}: {ai_err}")
                ai_analysis = None

        return jsonify({
            "status": "success",
            "identifier": identifier,
            "date": date_str,
            "user_info": vpn_data.get("user_info", {}),
            "summary": vpn_data.get("summary", {}),
            "device_telemetry": vpn_data.get("device_level_telemetry", [{}])[0] if vpn_data.get("device_level_telemetry") else {},
            "ai_analysis": ai_analysis,
        }), 200

    except http_requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
        logger.error(f"VPN Score API request failed: {e}")
        return jsonify({
            "status": "error",
            "message": f"VPN Score API unreachable: {str(e)}"
        }), 502
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error in get_vpn_score: {type(e).__name__}: {e}", exc_info=True)
        return jsonify({
            "status": "error",
            "message": f"Failed to retrieve VPN score: {str(e)}"
        }), 500


def _build_vpn_score_analysis_prompt(identifier, date_str, vpn_data):
    """Build the Gemini prompt for VPN score analysis."""
    user_info = vpn_data.get("user_info", {})
    summary = vpn_data.get("summary", {})
    device_telemetry = vpn_data.get("device_level_telemetry", [{}])[0] if vpn_data.get("device_level_telemetry") else {}

    penalties = device_telemetry.get("penalties", {})
    penalties_str = "\n".join([f"  - {k.replace('_', ' ').title()}: {v}" for k, v in penalties.items()]) if penalties else "  None reported"

    prompt = f"""You are a Network Security & Performance Analyst for an enterprise IT team. Analyze the following VPN health score data and provide actionable insights.

DEVICE: {identifier}
DATE: {date_str}
USER: {user_info.get('name', 'Unknown')} (EID: {user_info.get('eid', 'Unknown')})

VPN HEALTH SUMMARY:
- Overall VPN Score: {summary.get('overall_vpn_score', 'N/A')}
- Cohort Status: {summary.get('overall_cohort_label', 'Unknown')}
- Total Devices Reporting: {summary.get('total_devices_reporting', 'N/A')}

DEVICE-LEVEL TELEMETRY:
- Machine Name: {device_telemetry.get('machine_name', 'N/A')}
- VPN Score: {device_telemetry.get('user_vpn_score', 'N/A')}
- Cohort Label: {device_telemetry.get('cohort_label', 'N/A')}
- Active Hours Worked: {device_telemetry.get('active_hours_worked', 'N/A')}
- Hours in Frustration: {device_telemetry.get('hours_in_frustration', 'N/A')}
- Total Auth Blocks: {device_telemetry.get('total_auth_blocks', 'N/A')}
- Processed Timestamp: {device_telemetry.get('processed_timestamp', 'N/A')}

SCORING GUIDE (0-10 scale):
- 8-10: Excellent — no VPN issues
- 6-8: Acceptable — minor connectivity issues
- 4-6: Poor — noticeable VPN connectivity problems
- 0-4: Critical — severe VPN failures or authentication blocks

PENALTY BREAKDOWN (higher penalties indicate more problems):
{penalties_str}

PROVIDE YOUR ANALYSIS IN THIS EXACT FORMAT:

## Summary
(2-3 sentence overview of VPN health)

## Key Findings
(Bullet points of the most important observations about VPN stability and authentication issues)

## Problem Areas
(Identify which penalties are contributing most to the score reduction)

## Root Causes
(Based on penalty scores and auth blocks — explain likely causes: firewall, ISP issues, SAML auth problems, gateway congestion, etc.)

## Recommendations
(Actionable steps to improve VPN stability and reduce auth failures — be specific about which penalties to address)

Keep analysis concise and actionable. Focus on the highest-impact penalties and auth issues.
If the VPN score is high (8+), note that the device has excellent VPN connectivity.
"""
    return prompt

# DWP PULSE REPORT — AI-Powered Weekly Channel Analysis & Reporting
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Generates structured feedback reports from Slack channel conversations
# using Vertex AI (Gemini 2.5 Flash) + BigQuery + PostgreSQL.
#
# Flow:
#   1. POST /pulse-report/generate → fetch messages from BQ → Gemini analysis
#   2. Store results in PostgreSQL (dwppulse DB)
#   3. POST /pulse-report/send-email → sends HTML report via /send-email-dont-reply
#   4. Cloud Scheduler → cloudjob/main.py → calls generate + send weekly

def _get_pulse_db():
    """Get a PostgreSQL connection to the dwppulse database."""
    if PULSE_DB_UNIX_SOCKET:
        return pg8000.connect(
            user=PULSE_DB_USER,
            password=PULSE_DB_PASSWORD,
            database=PULSE_DB_NAME,
            unix_sock=f"{PULSE_DB_UNIX_SOCKET}/.s.PGSQL.5432",
        )
    return pg8000.connect(
        host=PULSE_DB_HOST,
        port=PULSE_DB_PORT,
        user=PULSE_DB_USER,
        password=PULSE_DB_PASSWORD,
        database=PULSE_DB_NAME,
    )


# ── Default Pulse Channels (seeded on first request) ────────────
PULSE_DEFAULT_CHANNELS = [
    {"channel_id": "C0AE1G97QCV", "channel_name": "help-gemini-google-workspace", "description": "Help Gemini Google Workspace"},
    {"channel_id": "C0AH7K4QXCY", "channel_name": "help-notebooklm", "description": "Help NotebookLM"},

    {"channel_id": "C0643RAS796", "channel_name": "help-gemini-google-workspace", "description": "Help Gemini Google Workspace"},
    {"channel_id": "C08QT7UL8TV", "channel_name": "C08QT7UL8TV", "description": ""},
    {"channel_id": "C05CHPFT57A", "channel_name": "digital-workplace-influencers", "description": "Digital Workplace Influencers"},
    {"channel_id": "C014NGFR1NV", "channel_name": "help-slack", "description": "Help Slack"},
